# THE EXAM DOCKET — Deuteronomy 12:1-31 (the header and the demolition — the statutes and the judgments to keep in the land, destroy the places on the mountains and the hills and under every leafy tree, the altars, the pillars, the Asherim, the images, their name; you shall not do so to the LORD your God; the place chosen — His name put there, His dwelling sought, the burnt offerings, the sacrifices, the tithes, the heave offering of the hand, the vows, the freewill offerings, the firstlings, eat there and rejoice, not as we do here today, the rest and the inheritance not yet come to, the Levite within your gates; the burnt offerings only there — take heed lest in every place you see, in one of your tribes; the profane slaughter, the blood and the gates — with all the desire of your soul, the unclean and the clean, as the gazelle and as the hart, the blood poured on the earth like water, not within your gates the tithe, the firstlings, the vows, the freewill offerings, the heave offering, before the LORD you shall eat it and rejoice, the Levite not forsaken; the border enlarged and the altar — as He has spoken, the place too far, slaughter as I have commanded you, the blood is the life, the life not eaten with the flesh, that it may go well with you, your holy things and your vows taken up to the place, the flesh and the blood on the altar, observe and hear all these words, the good and the right; the nations cut off and the abomination — lest you be ensnared, lest you inquire after their gods, every abomination which He hates, their sons and their daughters in the fire) — THE DEUTERONOMY WALK sitting 10b, THE DOCKET'S THREE RUNS (2026-09-20/21; DEUTERONOMY_WALK.md "Sitting 10b — THE COMPILE OF CHAPTER 12 … THE DESIGN", THE ORDER)

## Bekhorot
- Bekhorot 15a:1 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Rav's phrasing of the mishna's attribution — the blemished-before-consecration animal redeemable by all: F4 the_profane_slaughter_the_blood_and_the_gates — the blemished consecrated (Mishnah Bekhorot 2:2 carried; 12:15's release read on it).
- Bekhorot 15a:2 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Rav per R. Shimon ben Lakish — Temple-maintenance animals in standing and valuation, the altar's not; the latter clause 'if they died, buried': F4.
- Bekhorot 15a:3 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. why buried — standing and valuation, or no redemption to feed dogs? the tereifa (mortally wounded) case the proof: F4.
- Bekhorot 15a:4 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. or Rav per R. Yochanan — the altar's animals in standing and valuation, the mishna the Rabbis': F4.
- Bekhorot 15a:6 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. the baraita: the blemished consecrated excluded from the firstborn's mitzva (commandment) and from the gifts by 'as you eat the gazelle and the hart' (12:15; 15:22) — the hart exempt from both, so they: F4 — 12:22's comparison read on the redeemed animal.
- Bekhorot 15a:7 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. then their fat permitted like the hart's? 'however' differentiates — the analogy bounded to the firstborn and the gifts: F4 — the comparison's limit.
- Bekhorot 15a:8 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. why the firstborn first — it applies to males only, the gifts to all; 'a hart' added excludes the gifts too: F4.
- Bekhorot 15a:9 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Rav Pappa: then a mother and its offspring (Leviticus 22:28) — not for the gazelle, so not for these?: F4.
- Bekhorot 15a:10 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Abaye: compared to non-sacred or to sacrificial, the same-day ban applies to both — no exclusion derivable: F4 (same_day_slaughter_barred by kind).
- Bekhorot 15a:11 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Rav Pappa: say the same of the fat — yet the baraita derives it from 'however': F4.
- Bekhorot 15a:12 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. 'however' excludes both the fat and the same-day ban from the comparison; the juxtaposition of the animals named — the blemished consecrated at least like sacrificial animals: F4.
- Bekhorot 15a:13 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. Rava: 'however' the same-day ban; the fat from 'its blood' (15:23 'only you shall not eat its blood'): F4 — 15:23 the twin of 12:16 ahead.
- Bekhorot 15a:14 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. 'its blood' — the actual blood needs no verse (who permits the gazelle's blood?); so 'its blood' is its fat: F4 — THE BLOOD BAN UNIVERSAL (sanctions.blood('ban') by CALL); the exam's row.
- Bekhorot 15a:15 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. why not write 'fat'? — then the juxtaposition and the verse would each work in part: F4.

- Bekhorot 15a:17 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. 'its blood' tells you: as its blood is karet (excision), so its fat — the juxtaposition not applying: F4 — the blood's karet the measure (sanctions.blood('karet_own_eating') by CALL).
- Bekhorot 15a:18 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. but the baraita's tanna (the Mishnah-era teacher) read 'however' for the fat — Rava's source differs?: F4.

- Bekhorot 15b:10 [TOPIC] — LAW. misuse before the redemption, not after; the offspring non-sacred if born after, redeemable unblemished if before, dedicated as any offering; 'like non-sacred animals in all matters — only the mitzva (commandment) of value': F4 — the first row continued.
- Bekhorot 15b:11 [TOPIC] — LAW. the consecration BEFORE the blemish (or a temporary blemish then a permanent), redeemed — exempt from the firstborn and the gifts; lashes for shearing or working; a substitute made: F4 — THE TABLE'S SECOND ROW (Mishnah Bekhorot 2:3 carried); the exam's row.
- Bekhorot 15b:12 [TOPIC] — LAW. misuse before, not after; the offspring sacred, not redeemed unblemished, not dedicated freely; 'like redeemed sacrificial animals in all matters — only the permission of consumption': F4 — 12:15's release IS 'the permission of consumption alone'; the exam's row.
- Bekhorot 15b:13 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. 'the principle of the matter' adds — the first clause: slaughtered outside the courtyard EXEMPT (sanctions.outside by CALL); the second: (continues): F4.
- Bekhorot 16a:1 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. — its milk forbidden: F4.
- Bekhorot 16a:2 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the inferences — blemished offspring redeemable; dedicated as the mother's own offering: F4.
- Bekhorot 16a:3 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. both at once — the unborn dedicated as the mother's offering, redeemed when blemished; a refutation of Rav Huna?: F4.
- Bekhorot 16a:4 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Rav Huna: the clauses phrased for symmetry — no inference: F4.
- Bekhorot 16a:5 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Rav Huna's text: slaughtered outside LIABLE to karet (excision) — a minor blemish (the cornea), per R. Akiva (ascended, not descended): F3 the_burnt_offerings_only_there — the outside slaughter's karet on the potentially fit (sanctions.outside('fitness_path') by CALL).
- Bekhorot 16a:6 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Rav Nachman: its substitute after the redemption must die — neither sacrificed nor redeemed: F5 — the substitute's dead end.
- Bekhorot 16a:7 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Rav Amram: let it be eaten blemished like the firstborn's and the tithe's substitute (Mishnah Temurah 3:5 — 21a): F5 — 12:26's mishna named at its kin.
- Bekhorot 16a:8 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Abaye: each substitute bears its mother's name — the firstborn's eaten blemished: F5.
- Bekhorot 16a:9 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the redeemed animal's substitute eaten only through redemption, and it cannot be redeemed: F5.
- Bekhorot 16a:10 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. the baraita per Rav Nachman — Leviticus 11:4's 'impure to you': an animal with the signs yet forbidden, the disqualified consecrated's substitute; the five sin offerings from 'split hoof': F5.
- Bekhorot 16a:11 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the five sin offerings that die a tradition (a law given to Moses at Sinai), not a verse; the verse for the guilt offering's substitute grazing: F5.
- Bekhorot 16a:12 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. that too a tradition (the sin offering dies, the guilt offering grazes): F5.
- Bekhorot 16a:13 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. both needed — the verse alone would say graze, the tradition alone a prohibition without lashes; the verse makes it a negative mitzva (commandment): F5.
- Bekhorot 16a:14 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. or the verse juxtaposes the substitutes (from 'chew the cud') to the five (from 'split hoof') — they die; the juxtaposition named: F5.
- Bekhorot 16a:15 [TOPIC] — OUTSIDE. MISHNAH BEKHOROT 2:4's opening — animals received from a gentile as a guaranteed investment (continues past the range): the firstborn's exemption on a gentile's share.
- Bekhorot 33a:2 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. the a-fortiori (I1) refuted — the impure priest's ban is permitted for the communal service, the non-priest's never: F4 — the eaters of the blemished firstborn (33a:1 carried).
- Bekhorot 33a:3 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. Beit Hillel: the inference is on the EATING, not the service — the non-priest's eating superior: F4 — I1's refutation answered.
- Bekhorot 33a:4 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. R. Akiva's reason — 'like the gazelle and like the hart' (15:22): a gentile eats the gazelle, so the blemished firstborn: F4 — THE COMPARISON'S THIRD SEAT (12:15, 12:22, 15:22); the exam's row.
- Bekhorot 33a:6 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. the other verse — the gazelle exempt from the firstborn ('of your herd and of your flock', 15:19), so the disqualified consecrated (14a): F4.

- Bekhorot 33a:8 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. Beit Hillel: the breast-and-thigh comparison is the UNBLEMISHED firstborn's meat; the blemished's — 'the impure and the pure may eat it' (15:22): F4 — 12:15's 'the unclean and the clean' at its twin.
- Bekhorot 33a:9 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. Beit Shammai: the impure from an outside source (a corpse, a creeping animal) eats it; the impure from his own body (one with a discharge, a menstruant) not: F4 — 'the unclean' bounded.
- Bekhorot 33a:10 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. their proof — the Paschal offering in impurity (95b): those with a discharge, lepers and menstruants excluded; only corpse-impurity overridden: F4.
- Bekhorot 33a:11 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. Beit Hillel: there 'impure by a corpse' (Numbers 9:10) is specified; here 'the impure' unspecified — no difference: F4 — 12:15's UNQUALIFIED 'the unclean'; the exam's row.

- Bekhorot 33a:14 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. for the disqualified consecrated: R. Elazar son of R. Shimon — the two sin offerings, one blemished: F4.

- Bekhorot 33a:16 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. R. Elazar son of R. Shimon: even cooking in the pot — forbidden; the disqualified keep a measure of sanctity after slaughter: F4.

- Temurah 17b:3 [TOPIC] — LAW. MISHNAH TEMURAH 3:1 — the offspring and the substitutes of peace offerings, to the end of time, like peace offerings (hands laid, libations, the breast and thigh waved): F5 the_border_enlarged_and_the_altar — 12:26's 'your holy things … you shall take up and go' (17b:15); the answer sheet's row.
- Temurah 17b:4 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. why 'until the end of all time' — against R. Elazar's next mishna (the offspring left to die): F5.
- Temurah 17b:5 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. the source — Leviticus 3:1's needless 'if male if female': 'male' includes the offspring: F5.
- Temurah 17b:6 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. could it be by a-fortiori (I1) from the substitute, not grown from consecrated property?: F5.
- Temurah 17b:7 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. refuted — substitution applies to every sacrificial animal, offspring not (the males); so 'male' the offspring, 'female' the substitute: F5 — I1 refuted, the verse needed.
- Temurah 17b:8 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. the blemished animals' offspring and substitute from 'if male' / 'if female': F5.
- Temurah 17b:9 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Rav Safra: reverse them? Abaye: each from the same word as its unblemished kin: F5.
- Temurah 17b:10 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Rav Safra's real question — the whole verse reversed; Abaye: offspring a masculine noun, substitute a feminine: F5.
- Temurah 17b:11 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. for what — Shmuel: sacrificed on the altar (per R. Elazar: a female set aside for a burnt offering bearing a male — 18b): F5.
- Temurah 17b:12 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. the baraita needed beyond R. Elazar — no blemished peace offering exists, yet its offspring is sacrificed: F5.
- Temurah 17b:13 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. bar Padda: only to graze until blemished, redeemed for a burnt offering — all opinions; Rava (the altar) against Rav Pappa (grazing) the same: F5.
- Temurah 17b:15 [TOPIC] — LAW. 'Only your holy things' (12:26) — THE SUBSTITUTES; 'that you have' — THE OFFSPRING; 'you shall take up and go to the place': brought to the Temple: F5 the_border_enlarged_and_the_altar — 12:26'S WORDS PARSED (the Sifrei 77:5-6's reading at its Bavli seat); the exam's row.
- Temurah 17b:16 [TOPIC] — LAW. lest one bring them and starve them: 'you shall offer your burnt offerings, the flesh and the blood, on the altar' (12:27) — the burnt offering's substitute as a burnt offering, the peace offering's offspring and substitute as a peace offering: F5 — 12:27 THE RULE OF THEIR TREATMENT; the exam's row.
- Temurah 17b:17 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. for every offering? 'Only' excludes some — R. Yishmael; R. Akiva: 'only' needless, 'it is a guilt offering' (Leviticus 5:19) — it, not its substitute: F5 — 12:26's 'only' the exclusion's seat (R. Yishmael) or needless (R. Akiva).
- Temurah 17b:18 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the Master's clause restated — brought and left to die?: F5.
- Temurah 17b:19 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. whence — the five sin offerings by tradition; these sacrificed: F5.
- Temurah 17b:20 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the verse needed lest the peace offering's be left to die in the Temple specifically: F5.
- Temurah 17b:21 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. whose offspring does 'only' exclude — the burnt offering is male; the sin offering's by tradition (continues past the range): F5.

## The finds (this docket's crowns — the three runs D1, D2a, D2b)
- THE RECEIPT WITHOUT THE NAME'S REFERENT IS THE ORAL LAW (Chullin 28a:5, 85b:16, 86a:3; Mishnah Chullin 2:1): Rabbi on 12:21 "as I have commanded you" — Moses was commanded on the gullet and the windpipe, the majority of one sign for a bird and of two for an animal; the_rite_of_slaughter's VALUE from the answer sheet; against it R. Elazar HaKappar's bird by the scribes from 12:22's comparison (28a:4); WHICH SIGN FOR A BIRD a parameter (28a:6 — either / the gullet; 28a:13), THE UNCERTAINTY RULE (28a:8), the interruption and the deficiency (28a:11-12); everyone, always, with anything that cuts (Chullin 16b:13, 17a:16).
- THE WILDERNESS FLESH'S ARMS NARRATED, THE DISTANCE THEIR GROUND (Chullin 16b:14-17, 17a:2-4, 17a:10; 16b:16): R. Yishmael — the flesh of desire forbidden in the wilderness and eaten only as an offering, RELEASED at the entry, the reason THE DISTANCE from God's table (12:21's "too far"); R. Akiva — stabbing permitted, the rite REQUIRED at the entry; the two values named ("the flesh of desire never forbidden" / "the flesh of stabbing never permitted"); THE EXILE A THIRD STATE — "one may always slaughter" (16b:16, 17a:3); the wild animal outside the ban (17a:10 — 12:22's gazelle): the_wilderness_flesh's arms.
- THE STATIONS AT THEIR FOLIO (Zevachim 112b:9-12; 112b:13-15, 119b:10-12; 112b:16-113a:2, 119b:13-20; Mishnah Zevachim 14:4-8): the six rows of the table as the eras table holds them; the karet (excision) matrix and R. Shimon's four principles; the eleven differences and their sources; 12:8 read as THE VOLUNTARY OFFERINGS (117b:3 — "fitting in his eyes") and as A PROHIBITION for the premature (114a:13); the Gilgal row a parameter with five arms (117a:8-117b:1; the Paschal only on the great altar 114b:7; the appearance offering 118a:11); THE ERAS' DURATIONS 39 / 14 / 369 / 57 to the 480th year (118b:13-119a:2 — Seder Olam 11 at its Bavli seat); THE TWO NOUNS OF 12:9 FOUR WAYS (119a:9-13, 119b:1-5 — the rest Shiloh and the inheritance Jerusalem, or the reverse, or both one; the ark's rest by Numbers 10:36; the gap for Nob and Gibeon 119a:4); the second tithe at Nob and Gibeon disputed (119a:7-8); THE PORTION OF THE PLACE (118b:6-12).
- SHILOH AND JERUSALEM AT THE SECOND ANSWER SHEET, AND THE SANCTITY AFTER THE TEMPLE (Mishnah Megillah 1:10-11 at Megillah 9b:15-10a:1; 10a:2-9; Makkot 19a:8): the great altar and the small differ in the Paschal (the vowed and the voluntary on a small one); Shiloh's lesser holy things and second tithe eaten wherever it is seen, Jerusalem's within the walls; after Shiloh the improvised altars permitted, after Jerusalem never — THE INHERITANCE FOREVER; R. Yitzchak's Onias ruling RETRACTED before Mishnah Zevachim 14:8's "everlasting inheritance" (10a:4-5); the first consecration for its time or forever a two-arm parameter (R. Eliezer / R. Yehoshua, 10a:6-7; the walled cities 10a:9).
- THE ONE PLACEMENT AND THE ONE APPLICATION; THE BONES AND THE SINEWS; NO BLOOD NO FLESH (Zevachim 36b:20-37a:7, Sanhedrin 4a:7; 86a:1-4, Mishnah Zevachim 9:5-7; 104a:4): 12:27's "shall be poured" read by R. Yishmael and R. Akiva; the bones and the sinews reconciled; R. Yehoshua's rule for the altar; the export's empty rows Zevachim 83a:14, Temurah 17b:2, Keritot 20b:6 noted.
- THE LADDER OF A FORTIORI AT ITS BAVLI SEAT (Makkot 17a:12-17b:1; 17b:3-8; 17b:9; 17b:10; 18a:1-2; Keritot 4b:29-5a:2; Mishnah Makkot 3:3 at 17a:7): R. Shimon's five rungs on 12:17's list — the first fruits before the recitation, the peace offering before the sprinkling, the firstborn to a non-priest, the sin offering outside the courtyard, the burnt offering's flesh at all — each item a distinct liability because the lesser case already covered the wall; Rava's refutation of every rung and his praise: THE LIST READ BACKWARD (17b:9 — from the first fruits first); a mere prohibition from an inference, no lashes (17b:10); Rava's five sets on the non-priest (18a:1-2 credited); THE BAR'S ONSET at the wall (19b:15 — 12:18 read with 12:17).
- THE IMPURE TITHE'S WARNING FROM THE CHAPTER'S OWN GATES (Makkot 19b:3-7; 19b:4; 19b:9-13): the pure tithe outside the wall here, the impure inside Jerusalem there; R. Shimon's "I have not put any of it away when impure" (26:14) with no warning seen — the school of R. Yishmael supplies it from 12:15's "the impure … within your gates" against 12:17's "you may not eat within your gates"; THE SECOND TITHE AND THE TEMPLE (19a:3-5 — juxtaposed to the firstborn in 14:23, only while it stands; 19a:7 the common element refuted; 19a:10 the chain rule for non-sacred matters); "TOO FAR" AS ANY INABILITY (19b:9-11 — "unable to carry it" is eating; one stride outside the wall; "from you" from your fullness, 19b:13).
- THE BLEMISHED CONSECRATED'S TABLE AT ITS FOLIO (Bekhorot 15b:9-12; 15a:6-8; 15a:13-17; 15b:1; 33b:20; Mishnah Bekhorot 2:2-3): Rav Sheshet's baraita — the blemish before the consecration: like non-sacred in all matters, only the mitzva (commandment) of value; the consecration before the blemish: like redeemed sacrificial animals, ONLY THE PERMISSION OF CONSUMPTION — 12:15's release read on the redeemed animal; the firstborn and the gifts excluded by "as the gazelle and the hart" with "however" bounding the analogy; the fat from "its blood" (15:23) so that its karet (excision) stands; "you may slaughter and eat" — permission from the slaughter; the remaining bans after the redemption.
- THE EATERS OF THE BLEMISHED FIRSTBORN (Bekhorot 33a:1-11): R. Akiva — a gentile eats it as he eats the gazelle (33a:4, the comparison's third seat: 12:15, 12:22, 15:22); Beit Shammai's impure-from-his-own-body against Beit Hillel's UNQUALIFIED "the impure" — unlike Numbers 9:10's "impure by a corpse" (33a:8-11): 12:15's "the unclean and the clean" without a qualifier.
- 12:26'S WORDS PARSED (Temurah 17b:15-17; Mishnah Temurah 3:1 at 17b:3): "only your holy things" THE SUBSTITUTES, "that you have" THE OFFSPRING, "you shall take up and go" brought to the Temple; 12:27 the rule of their treatment (the burnt offering's substitute as a burnt offering, the peace offering's as a peace offering); "only" R. Yishmael's exclusion or needless for R. Akiva.
- THE CIVILITY, THE PARTITIVE "OF", AND "LIKE WATER" BOUNDED (Chullin 84a:18-21, 84a:24, 84b:4; 84a:10-16; Pesachim 22a:8; Chullin 33a:12; 35b:15; Mishnah Makhshirin 6:4): meat as if trapped; 12:21's "OF your cattle" some, not all; R. Elazar ben Azaria's ladder of means; no covering for the domestic animal but no immersion in blood (Leviticus 11:36's three waters), benefit, susceptibility, the disqualified consecrated's blood — four readings and a fifth refused.
- THE BLOOD'S CLASSES (Mishnah Keritot 5:1 at Keritot 20b:7-8; 20b:9-21a:8; 21a:10-11; 21b:6; 21b:11; 22a:4; 22a:10; 22a:17-19; Yevamot 47b credited): the blood with which the soul departs of a domestic animal, a wild animal or a bird — karet (excision); the spleen's, the heart's, the exudate's not; the species by Leviticus 7:26's general-particular-general (I6 — a light and a severe impurity, forbidden-or-permitted, a type of meat; R. Yishmael's school on unlike generalizations); the koy (the creature of uncertain class) included, its carcass by 14:21's "any"; THE WILD ANIMAL'S BLOOD UNDER THE BAN (12:22's gazelle); THE THREE CLASSES karet / lashes / no liability; HUMAN BLOOD OUTSIDE 12:16's "the blood" ("these are impure to you" — Rav Sheshet); THE LIFE-BLOOD'S BOUNDARY (while it spurts / from the last black drop); THE EXUDATE'S KARET WITHOUT ATONEMENT (12:23's "the blood is the life" beside Leviticus 17:11's "by reason of the soul").
- THE LIMB FROM THE LIVING AND FLESH IN MILK (Chullin 102b:1, 102b:11-13, 103a:4-17; 115b:1, 115b:2; 113a:18-19, 113b:5-8, 114b:9-10, 115a:10, 115b:4-7, 116a:7-8, 115b:13-116a:2, 116a:12-14): the limb's measure, its source divided, the lashes' count and the stacking rule (Leviticus 7:24 the teacher); FLESH IN MILK FROM 12:25'S REDUNDANT "YOU SHALL NOT EAT IT" and RABBI'S READING DEFENDED BY THE CHAPTER'S OWN CONTEXT — "a matter derived from its context" (I12): the adjacent verses the redeemed consecrated, two types, so a prohibition of two types; Mishnah Chullin 8:4 at its folio, Shmuel's six rules (THE CHAPTER'S BLOOD NOT MEAT for the milk law), the eating and the benefit bans' sources, the refutation rules, the wild animal and the bird in milk the arms.
- THE NAME PRONOUNCED ONLY THERE (Sotah 38a:10 credited; 38a:11; Mishnah Sotah 7:6 at 38a:1): "to put His name there" (12:5) with Numbers 6:27 by analogy, and R. Yoshiya's second source — Exodus 20:20 TRANSPOSED: where I come and bless you, there My name — the Temple, the Name not mentioned elsewhere; the Name as written in the Temple, its substitute in the country.
- THE THREE COMMANDMENTS OF THE ENTRY AND THE REST BEFORE THE HOUSE (Sanhedrin 20b:8-13; 20b:14; 90a:3): the king, Amalek, the Chosen House in order — "the throne of the LORD" a king first; David's rest from all his enemies (2 Samuel 7:1) and then the seeking of the site (7:2): 12:10-11's order in the run; THE DEMOLITION A POSITIVE COMMAND (90a:3); name_erasure_barred's readings (Avodah Zarah 13b:3; Pesachim 48a:7; Sanhedrin 113a:3, 71a:17; Shabbat 120b:10; Temurah 28b:7); Mishnah Avodah Zarah 3:6 at its folio (47a:21).
- THE DELAY'S CLOCK AND THE WOMAN'S REJOICING (Rosh Hashanah 6b:3, 6b:8-11, 6b:13-14, 6b:15-16, 6b:17; 4a-6a credited): "you shall not delay" on 12:6's vows and firstlings — a year or three Festivals; Shavuot's date floating with the months' lengths (6b:10); the heir excluded by "from you"; THE WOMAN BOUND TO REJOICE (12:7, 12, 18's household) — the rejoicing HER obligation (R. Zeira, Abaye) or her husband's to make her joyful: a two-arm parameter beside Mishnah Yoma 1:1's "his house is his wife"; the firstborn's year from birth or from fitness.
- THE PERSECUTION'S EDGES (Sanhedrin 74a-b — 74b:1, 74b:5-7, 74b:8-10): even a sandal's strap in a time of persecution; a gentile's own pleasure not persecution (Esther; the coal shovels); the Noahide and the seven with THE LIMB OF A LIVING ANIMAL among them (74b:9 — 12:23's limb, limb_barred by kind); Naaman's bowing forgiven (74b:10).
- THE LASHES AND KARET, THE BLOOD'S REWARD, THE 613 (Makkot 23b:2 credited; 23b:4-7; 23b:18; Mishnah Makkot 3:15-16): the blood's reward by a-fortiori; whether the flogged are exempt from karet (excision) — R. Chananya ben Gamliel's arm the halakha (the ruling) per Rav; R. Simlai's 613 with 33:4's letter-values (E29).
- THE DOCKET'S OWN FORM (this sitting): the credited rows CARRIED with their ledgers' own verdict lines (ch12_credit_carry.py — 423 of 540 resolved; 117 read whole here where the form carried none, split by run); the chunker flushing at the runs' boundaries {582, 933} (the boundary CROSSED, not hit); the 76,000-byte chunk read in two pages where it overshot the page; THREE carried rows in part G (Pesachim 8b:6-8) whose ledger line was itself an earlier docket's read-whole note — counted once as carried, the writer's unresolved count taken from the carry's own file.
- WHAT THE DESIGN DID NOT PREDICT (carried to RUN B as DATA rows or values): the receipt's referent the oral law by name (the_rite_of_slaughter's value from Mishnah Chullin 2:1); the exile a third state of the_wilderness_flesh; the distance the ground of the release; the impure tithe's warning from the chapter's own gates; the list read backward; "too far" as any inability; the woman's rejoicing a two-arm parameter; human blood outside the ban and the exudate's karet without atonement; the wild animal's blood under the ban; Rabbi's 12:25 defended by the context (I12); Shavuot's floating date; the Onias ruling retracted before the answer sheet; the eras' durations and the two nouns four ways; the export's empty rows (Zevachim 83a:14, Temurah 17b:2, Keritot 20b:6) and its empty chapters (Tosefta Zevachim 4, Tosefta Menachot 9, Seder Olam 11).
